Six test groups pass under both local Python runtimes, and thirty-two static entry-point checks pass. The deterministic fictional registers test initial retrieval, an explicit owner/budget/status correction, unchanged handoff information, and a subsequent unrelated project update. The grader requires exact typed structured answers; it rejects duplicate keys, wrong values/types and nonterminal replies. Actual assistant answers remain in history, so a later correct response cannot erase an earlier wrong one. A trusted reply-reservation refusal is an outcome failure; crashes and invalid native events propagate as execution failures. No tool actions occur.

Nine prospective cases place the queried record at the start, middle and end of each register size. The frozen native tokenizer produces first-turn input lengths 2086 to 2093, 7990 to 8005, and 28668 to 28705 tokens. This is input geometry only. The native command takes the reviewed prepare-only branch before model ownership or allocation, under a separate one-GB physical ceiling and ninety-second deadline. Its sampled peak is 173769448 bytes and it completes successfully in 1.068227667012252 seconds. These are instrument resource observations, not inference performance. The running held-out model campaign remains frozen and serial.

Prepared first-turn token IDs, exact source cases and every artifact digest are preserved below. They do not constitute model answers, quality acceptance, a final execution protocol or statistical long-context noninferiority. Before inference, freeze the session settings, worst-case continuation/reply reservations, execution cost and acceptance rule; verify actual native admission on every turn. A separately completed conversation gate must retain all original/candidate outcomes. The final general-task campaign, app basics, image outcomes and complete performance remain independent.

Home paths are normalized to <HOME>. The original and normalized digests identify every output. Embedded text is compressed losslessly and roundtrip-checked; no weight files or logits are copied into this record.
